widely regarded as one of the largest and most ambitious content mods ever created for *Terraria*. Functioning almost like an official sequel, it completely transforms the game by adding thousands of new items, dozens of challenging bosses, entirely new biomes, and a reworked progression system that extends far beyond the vanilla game's endpoint.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### Key Features\\n\\n* **Massive Expansion \\u0026 Post-Moon Lord Endgame:** Defeating the vanilla game's final boss, the Moon Lord, only covers roughly 60% of Calamity's overall progression. The mod introduces over 25 unique bosses—culminating in monumental super-bosses like the Supreme Witch, Calamitas.\\n* **The Rogue Class:** Calamity introduces a fully fleshed-out fifth playable class. It features a unique **Stealth mechanic**: refraining from attacking builds up a meter, which can then be unleashed via powerful \\\"Stealth Strikes\\\".\\n* **New Biomes \\u0026 Structures:** Players can explore dangerous environments like the **Abyss** (a deep, hazardous underwater trench requiring specialized gear), the **Astral Infection** (a space-blighted biome spawned after defeating the Wall of Flesh), the Sunken Sea, and the Brimstone Crag.\\n* **Advanced Difficulties:** For players seeking an intense challenge, the mod features custom difficulty settings like **Revengeance** and **Death** modes. Functioning almost like an official sequel, it completely transforms the game by adding thousands of new items, dozens of challenging bosses, entirely new biomes, and a reworked progression system that extends far beyond the vanilla game's endpoint.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### Key Features\\n\\n* **Massive Expansion \\u0026 Post-Moon Lord Endgame:** Defeating the vanilla game's final boss, the Moon Lord, only covers roughly 60% of Calamity's overall progression. The mod introduces over 25 unique bosses—culminating in monumental super-bosses like the Supreme Witch, Calamitas.\\n* **The Rogue Class:** Calamity introduces a fully fleshed-out fifth playable class. It features a unique **Stealth mechanic**: refraining from attacking builds up a meter, which can then be unleashed via powerful \\\"Stealth Strikes\\\".\\n* **New Biomes \\u0026 Structures:** Players can explore dangerous environments like the **Abyss** (a deep, hazardous underwater trench requiring specialized gear), the **Astral Infection** (a space-blighted biome spawned after defeating the Wall of Flesh), the Sunken Sea, and the Brimstone Crag.\\n* **Advanced Difficulties:** For players seeking an intense challenge, the mod features custom difficulty settings like **Revengeance** and **Death** modes. These alter enemy AI, rework vanilla bosses, and introduce dynamic mechanics like **Adrenaline** and **Rage** meters that reward skillful, hitless play.\\n* **Acclaimed Soundtrack:** Calamity features an expansive, professionally produced original orchestral and electronic soundtrack (largely composed originally by DM Dokuro) that has achieved independent acclaim outside of the game.\\n\\n### How to Play\\nBecause of its massive scope, the mod requires **tModLoader** (available directly via Steam).
